## Contradictions

- Stored Token Services product page prints the token endpoint as https://apis.discover.com/auth/OAuth 2/v2/token (with a space) · versus · Going Live developer guide prints https://apis.discover.com/auth/oauth/v2/token · https://partner.discoverglobalnetwork.com/products · https://partner.discoverglobalnetwork.com/going-live-with-discover
- developer.discover.com/explore-apis shows seven products logged out, including HCE Wallet Services, City Guides and ATM Locator · versus · partner.discoverglobalnetwork.com/products shows twelve products logged out with none of those three, but with Stored Token Services, IIN Data Lookup, Account Updater, iCAS, PAR, Fraud Alerts · https://developer.discover.com/explore-apis · https://partner.discoverglobalnetwork.com/products
- FY2025 10-K defines Global Payment Network as Discover Network, PULSE Network, Diners Club International and Network Partners, collectively. · versus · Q2 2026 10-Q defines Global Payment Network as 'The Discover Network, PULSE Network and Diners Club, collectively' (Network Partners dropped from the definition). · https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/927628/000092762826000024/cof-20251231.htm · https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/927628/000092762826000089/cof-20260630.htm
- Home page: 'more than 439 million cards that run on our global network' (network participant data as of December 2025 plus Datos data as of 12/31/2024) · versus · Our Unique Network page: 'global acceptance to 378+ million cards worldwide' (network participants and RBR Data Services published as of 2024) · https://www.discoverglobalnetwork.com/ · https://www.discoverglobalnetwork.com/our-network/our-unique-network/
- Home page: 'Customers spent over $634B across Discover Network, Diners Club International, and PULSE Networks in 2025' (footnote says total volume in 2024 and 2025) · versus · FY2024 10-K: Total Network Transaction Volume $621,913 million for 2024 · https://www.discoverglobalnetwork.com/ · https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1393612/000139361225000009/dfs-20241231.htm
- DGN contact page names a 'Discover Network Relationship Manager' as the acquirer/issuer escalation contact for fraud questions. · versus · The same page's Transaction Processor section and the EASI login help name a 'Discover Global Network Account Executive' as the escalation point; titles for the account-management role are inconsistent across personas. · https://servicecenter.discoverglobalnetwork.com/onlineform/contact-us · https://www.discovereasi.com/
- Developer Center FAQ says new users get access by filling out the Contact Us form and a representative will be in touch, and that products appear after login. · versus · The same FAQ says developer partners can only be added with an invite from a Discover representative, and the login page exposes 'User Invite Success' states, i.e. invite-driven rather than self-service. · https://developer.discover.com/faq · https://developer.discover.com/home
